Let's now write final code. Ensure no trailing analysis.```python
"""
Search for low R(a)=2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a))^2 over non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
- Use the supplied parent when available and evaluate it.
- Generate diverse analytic / random initializations for several lengths.
- Fast smoothed max-convolution mirror descent (log-sum-exp / active peaks).
- Fast SCA via a matrix-game / multiplicative-weights approximation of the
  linearized subproblem, followed by exact line searches.
- Optional exact linearized minimax LP polish (HiGHS).
- Random basin hopping, symmetrization, and pairwise blending.

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, non-negative, and finite.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def lin_convolve(u, v):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(u)
    m = len(v)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = n + m - 1
    fft_size = next_pow2(size)
    U = np.fft.rfft(u, fft_size)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v, fft_size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(U * V, fft_size)[:size]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def fast_conv_same(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size)[:size]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def score_direct(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    an = a / s
    M = float(fast_conv_same(an).max())
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    l = len(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        if l == 0:
            return a
        return np.ones(l, dtype=np.float64) / float(l)
    return a / s


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -np.inf)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x - mx
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def soft_active_weights(conv, tau):
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64)
    if conv.size == 0:
        return conv
    if tau is None or tau <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    z = (conv - float(conv.max())) / tau
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weighted_corr(lam, f):
    """
    h[i] = sum_k lam[k] * f[k-i] over valid f indices.
    This is the derivative (up to factor 2) of lambda-weighted convolution.
    """
    L = len(lam)
    n = len(f)
    rev = lam[::-1]
    conv = lin_convolve(f, rev)
    # conv length is n + L - 1.  Needed indices are L-n, ..., L-1 reversed.
    return np.maximum(conv[L - n:L][::-1], 0.0)


def blend_line_search(f, g, curr_M, alphas=None):
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
    best_a = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_M = float(curr_M)
    best_conv = None
    for alpha in alphas:
        if alpha <= 0.0:
            continue
        a = (1.0 - alpha) * f + alpha * g
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a = a / s
        conv = fast_conv_same(a)
        M = float(conv.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_conv = conv
    if best_conv is None:
        best_conv = fast_conv_same(best_a)
        best_M = float(best_conv.max())
    return best_a, best_M, best_conv


def interpolate_sequence(parent, new_n):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if len(parent) == new_n:
        return parent.copy()
    if len(parent) == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(parent[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(parent))
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, parent)


def perturb_simplex(base, scale, seed):
    seed = int(seed) % 998244353
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    v = base + rng.standard_normal(len(base)) * scale
    return normalize_nonneg(v, 0.0)


def append_normalized(collection, v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        collection.append(v / s)
    return collection


def add_to_top(top, score, seq, max_keep=5):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    top.append((float(score), np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(top) > max_keep:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > top[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        top.pop(worst)


def try_candidate(state, a, label, force=False):
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    an = a / s
    conv = fast_conv_same(an)
    M = float(conv.max())
    direct = float(2.0 * an.size * M)
    if not np.isfinite(direct):
        return direct

    old_direct = state[3]
    if direct < state[3]:
        state[3] = direct

    do_eval = force or (state[0] is None) or (direct < old_direct - 1e-12)
    if do_eval:
        try:
            actual = float(evaluate_sequence(an.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            actual = direct
        if np.isfinite(actual) and (state[0] is None or actual < state[1]):
            state[0] = an.copy()
            state[1] = float(actual)
            state[2] = int(an.size)
            state[3] = direct
            print(f"NEW BEST [{label}] actual={state[1]:.8f} direct={direct:.8f} n={state[2]}")
        elif state[0] is None and np.isfinite(direct):
            state[0] = an.copy()
            state[1] = float(direct)
            state[2] = int(an.size)
            state[3] = direct
    return direct


def deterministic_candidates(n, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % 998244353)
    c = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    append_normalized(c, np.ones(n))

    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        append_normalized(c, (idx + 1.0) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (n - idx) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (idx + 1.0) ** p + 0.5 * (n - idx) ** p)

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        append_normalized(c, x ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (1.0 - x) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, x ** p + 0.5 * (1.0 - x) ** p)

    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7):
        for amp in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 0.99):
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + 0.5))

    for alpha in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
        append_normalized(c, rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)))

    for _ in range(6):
        k = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
        amp = 0.3 + 0.5 * float(rng.random())
        phase = float(rng.random() * 6.28318)
        v = (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + phase)
             + 0.3 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (k + 1) * x + phase))
        append_normalized(c, v)

    d = np.minimum(x, 1.0 - x) + 1e-8
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 / (d ** p))

    append_normalized(c, np.exp(-16.0 * (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5)))

    for period in (2, 3, 5, 8):
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * idx / period))
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * idx / period))

    append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 - 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.7 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 - 0.3 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    return c


def lse_mirror_optimize(a_init, time_limit, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.3,
                        seed=0, verbose=False):
    """Fast mirror descent with softmax/active-peak smoothing."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    tau = max(1e-12, tau_frac * M)
    ms = float(move_scale)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        it += 1
        p = soft_active_weights(conv, tau)
        h = weighted_corr(p, a)
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if hmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        eta = ms / hmax
        a_raw = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a_raw + 1e-12, 0.0)

        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(
            a, a_new, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
        )

        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                tau = max(1e-12, tau * 0.95)
                ms = min(1.0, ms * 1.02)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                tau = min(2.0 * M + 1e-12, tau * 1.05)
                ms = max(0.05, ms * 0.98)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            tau = min(2.0 * M + 1e-12, tau * 1.15)
            ms = max(0.05, ms * 0.90)
            if no_improve > 40:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 40) % 5)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                tau = max(1e-12, 0.1 * M)
                ms = 0.3
                no_improve = 0

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    LSE it={it} M={best_M:.10f}")

    return best, best_M


def game_sca_optimize(a_init, time_limit, temp_frac=0.2, inner_block=20,
                      beta=0.25, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    SCA outer iteration.  Inner matrix game approximates:
       min_{g in simplex} max_k (a*g)_k
    via multiplicative weights on payoff matrix F_{k,i}=a_{k-i}.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    size_g = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    size_l = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
    Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)

    g = a.copy()
    g_avg = a.copy()
    T = max(1e-12, float(temp_frac) * M)
    lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
    lam_avg = lam.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    outer = 0
    no_improve = 0
    alphas = (0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        for _ in range(max(1, int(inner_block))):
            Gg = np.fft.rfft(g, size_g)
            fg = np.fft.irfft(Af_g * Gg, size_g)[:L]
            row = softmax(fg / T)

            lamr = lam[::-1]
            Ll = np.fft.rfft(lamr, size_l)
            Rt = np.fft.irfft(Af_l * Ll, size_l)
            h = np.maximum(Rt[L - n:L][::-1], 0.0)

            col = softmax(-h / T)

            g = col
            lam = row
            lam_avg = (1.0 - beta) * lam_avg + beta * row
            g_avg = (1.0 - beta) * g_avg + beta * col

        g_use = normalize_nonneg(g_avg, 0.0)
        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, g_use, M, alphas=alphas)
        improved = False
        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            improved = True
        else:
            hmax = float(h.max())
            if hmax > 1e-300:
                eta = 0.2 / hmax
                a_mw = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(-eta * h) + 1e-12, 0.0)
                newa2, newM2, newconv2 = blend_line_search(
                    a, a_mw, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
                )
                if newM2 < M - 1e-15:
                    a, M, conv = newa2, newM2, newconv2
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and outer % 25 == 0:
                    print(f"    GAME outer={outer} M={best_M:.10f}")

            Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
            Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)
            temp_frac = max(0.05, float(temp_frac) * 0.95)
            T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)

            g = g_use.copy()
            g_avg = g_use.copy()
            lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
            lam_avg = lam.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            temp_frac = min(2.0, float(temp_frac) * 1.1)
            T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)
            if no_improve > 30:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.004 * (1 + (no_improve // 30) % 5)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + outer)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best = a.copy()
                Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
                Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)
                temp_frac = 0.2
                T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)
                g = a.copy()
                g_avg = a.copy()
                lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
                lam_avg = lam.copy()
                no_improve = 0

        outer += 1

    return best, best_M


def build_conv_structure(n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = n * n
    rows = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    p = 0
    for k in range(L):
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(n - 1, k)
        c = end - start + 1
        if c > 0:
            rows[p:p + c] = k
            cols[p:p + c] = np.arange(start, end + 1, dtype=np.int32)
            p += c
    return rows, cols


def sca_lp_minmax_step(f, row_idx, col_idx, t_rows, t_cols):
    n = int(len(f))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    vals = f[row_idx - col_idx]
    data = np.concatenate((vals, -np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)))
    rr = np.concatenate((row_idx, t_rows))
    cc = np.concatenate((col_idx, t_cols))
    A_ub = csr_matrix((data, (rr, cc)), shape=(L, n + 1))
    b_ub = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    cobj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cobj[-1] = 1.0

    A_eq = csr_matrix((np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), (np.arange(n), np.arange(n))),
                      shape=(1, n + 1))
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)

    res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                  bounds=bounds, method="highs", options={"presolve": True})
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
        g = np.maximum(res.x[:n], 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            return g / s
    return None


def sca_lp_optimize(a_init, time_limit, seed=0, max_elems=1800000, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n * n > max_elems:
        return a, score_direct(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    row_idx, col_idx = build_conv_structure(n)
    t_rows = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)
    t_cols = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)

    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        g = None
        try:
            g = sca_lp_minmax_step(a, row_idx, col_idx, t_rows, t_cols)
        except Exception:
            g = None

        improved = False
        if g is not None:
            g = normalize_nonneg(g, 0.0)
            newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, g, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0))
            if newM < M - 1e-15:
                a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            p = soft_active_weights(conv, max(1e-12, 0.05 * M))
            h = weighted_corr(p, a)
            hmax = float(h.max())
            if hmax > 1e-300:
                eta = 0.2 / hmax
                a_mw = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(-eta * h) + 1e-12, 0.0)
                newa2, newM2, newconv2 = blend_line_search(
                    a, a_mw, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
                )
                if newM2 < M - 1e-15:
                    a, M, conv = newa2, newM2, newconv2
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"    LP it={it} M={best_M:.10f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 8) % 4)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    return best, best_M


def peak_shave_optimize(a_init, time_limit, seed=0, frac=0.05, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        conv = fast_conv_same(a)
        M = float(conv.max())
        if M < best_M:
            best_M = M
            best = a.copy()

        k = int(np.argmax(conv))
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k)
        idx = np.arange(js, je + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if idx.size > 0:
            partner = k - idx
            contrib[idx] = 2.0 * a[idx] * a[partner]
        total = float(contrib.sum())
        if total <= 1e-300:
            break

        move = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        frac_cur = float(frac) * (1.0 + 0.05 * (it % 5))
        if idx.size > 0:
            move[idx] = -0.5 * frac_cur * a[idx] * (contrib[idx] / total)

        outside = np.concatenate((np.arange(0, js, dtype=np.int64),
                                  np.arange(je + 1, n, dtype=np.int64)))
        if outside.size == 0:
            avg = total / float(idx.size) if idx.size > 0 else 0.0
            low = idx[contrib[idx] < avg]
            if low.size == 0:
                no_improve += 1
                it += 1
                if no_improve > 20:
                    a = perturb_simplex(best, 0.003, seed + it)
                    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                    M = float(conv.max())
                    no_improve = 0
                continue
            svals = float(a[low].sum())
            if svals > 1e-300:
                move[low] = -float(move.sum()) * a[low] / svals
            else:
                move[low] = -float(move.sum()) / float(low.size)
        else:
            total_move = -float(move.sum())
            if total_move > 0.0:
                vals = a[outside]
                svals = float(vals.sum())
                if svals > 1e-300:
                    move[outside] = total_move * vals / svals
                else:
                    move[outside] = total_move / float(outside.size)

        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a + move, 0.0)
        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, a_new, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0))
        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 25) % 4)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    if M < best_M:
        best_M = M
        best = a.copy()
    return best, best_M


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 25.0
    seed = int(seed) % 998244353

    state = [None, float("inf"), 0, float("inf")]
    top = []

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
                parent = normalize_nonneg(p, 0.0)
                d = try_candidate(state, parent, "parent", force=True)
                add_to_top(top, d, parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n_list = []
    if parent is not None:
        n_list.append(int(parent.size))
    for n in (256, 384, 512, 640, 768, 896, 1024, 1280, 1536, 2048):
        n_list.append(n)

    unique_n = []
    for n in n_list:
        if 64 <= n <= 3072 and n not in unique_n:
            unique_n.append(int(n))
    unique_n.sort()

    for n in unique_n:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
            break
        if parent is not None:
            an = interpolate_sequence(parent, n)
        else:
            an = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        an = normalize_nonneg(an, 0.0)
        d = try_candidate(state, an, f"parent_n{n}")
        add_to_top(top, d, an)

        if n in (512, 640, 768, 896, 1024):
            cands = deterministic_candidates(n, seed + n)
            if len(cands) > 80:
                cands = cands[:80]
            for ci, cand in enumerate(cands):
                d = try_candidate(state, cand, f"cand_n{n}_{ci}")
                add_to_top(top, d, cand)
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
                    break
        else:
            for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 5.0):
                rng2 = np.random.default_rng((seed + 1000 * n + int(alpha * 100)) % 998244353)
                a = rng2.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha))
                d = try_candidate(state, a, "dir")
                add_to_top(top, d, a)

    if state[0] is None:
        a = np.ones(768, dtype=np.float64) / 768.0
        d = try_candidate(state, a, "uniform_fallback", force=True)
        add_to_top(top, d, a)

    # Symmetrize current top sequences.
    for _, a in list(top):
        s = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        s = normalize_nonneg(s, 0.0)
        d = try_candidate(state, s, "sym")
        add_to_top(top, d, s)

    # Pairwise blends among diverse top candidates.
    top_snapshot = list(top)
    for i in range(len(top_snapshot)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(top_snapshot)):
            a1 = top_snapshot[i][1]
            a2 = top_snapshot[j][1]
            if a1.shape != a2.shape:
                continue
            for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                b = alpha * a1 + (1.0 - alpha) * a2
                b = normalize_nonneg(b, 0.0)
                d = try_candidate(state, b, "blend")
                add_to_top(top, d, b)

    best_a = state[0].copy() if state[0] is not None else np.ones(768) / 768.0

    # Quick smooth mirror descent.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        t = min(80.0, rem - 250.0)
        if t > 10.0:
            res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(best_a, t, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.3,
                                           seed=seed, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lse1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Main SCA game optimization.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500.0:
        t = min(800.0, rem - 300.0)
        if t > 30.0:
            res_a, _ = game_sca_optimize(best_a, t, temp_frac=0.20,
                                         inner_block=20, beta=0.25,
                                         seed=seed + 10, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "game1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Second diverse basin, if available.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 250.0 and len(top) > 1:
        sec_a = top[1][1]
        t = min(150.0, rem - 150.0)
        if t > 20.0:
            res_a, _ = game_sca_optimize(sec_a, t, temp_frac=0.25,
                                         inner_block=20, beta=0.25,
                                         seed=seed + 20, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "game2", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Exact linearized LP polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        t = min(150.0, rem - 80.0)
        if t > 20.0:
            res_a, _ = sca_lp_optimize(best_a, t, seed=seed + 30, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lp1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Final smooth polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        t = min(60.0, rem - 30.0)
        if t > 10.0:
            res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(best_a, t, tau_frac=0.02, move_scale=0.15,
                                           seed=seed + 40, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lse2", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Peak shaving.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0:
        t = min(30.0, rem - 20.0)
        if t > 5.0:
            res_a, _ = peak_shave_optimize(best_a, t, seed=seed + 50, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "peak", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Random basin hopping.
    restart = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        rng_r = np.random.default_rng((seed + 5000 + restart) % 998244353)
        if restart % 4 == 0 and len(top) > 0:
            idxb = int(rng_r.integers(0, len(top)))
            base = top[idxb][1]
        else:
            base = best_a
        scale = 0.008 + 0.02 * float(int(rng_r.integers(0, 6)))
        a = perturb_simplex(base, scale, seed + 10000 + restart)
        t = min(10.0, rem - 5.0)
        if t < 5.0:
            break
        res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(a, t, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.25,
                                       seed=seed + 20000 + restart, verbose=False)
        d = try_candidate(state, res_a, f"restart{restart}", force=False)
        add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
        if state[0] is not None:
            best_a = state[0].copy()
        restart += 1
        if restart >= 40:
            break

    # Final blends after all optimizers.
    top_snapshot = list(top)
    for i in range(len(top_snapshot)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(top_snapshot)):
            a1 = top_snapshot[i][1]
            a2 = top_snapshot[j][1]
            if a1.shape != a2.shape:
                continue
            b = 0.5 * a1 + 0.5 * a2
            b = normalize_nonneg(b, 0.0)
            d = try_candidate(state, b, "final_blend", force=False)
            add_to_top(top, d, b)

    if state[0] is not None:
        try:
            final_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(state[0].tolist()))
        except Exception:
            final_actual = state[1]
        if np.isfinite(final_actual) and final_actual < state[1]:
            state[1] = float(final_actual)
        print(f"FINAL score={state[1]:.8f} n={state[2]}")
        return [float(x) for x in state[0]]

    return [1.0 / 768.0] * 768
```

```python
"""
Search for low R(a)=2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a))^2 over non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
- Use the supplied parent when available and evaluate it.
- Generate diverse analytic / random initializations for several lengths.
- Fast smoothed max-convolution mirror descent (active peaks / log-sum-exp style).
- Fast SCA via a matrix-game / multiplicative-weights approximation of the
  linearized subproblem, followed by exact line searches.
- Optional exact linearized minimax LP polish (HiGHS).
- Random basin hopping, symmetrization, and pairwise blending.

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, non-negative, and finite.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def lin_convolve(u, v):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(u)
    m = len(v)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = n + m - 1
    fft_size = next_pow2(size)
    U = np.fft.rfft(u, fft_size)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v, fft_size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(U * V, fft_size)[:size]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def fast_conv_same(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size)[:size]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def score_direct(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    an = a / s
    M = float(fast_conv_same(an).max())
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    l = len(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        if l == 0:
            return a
        return np.ones(l, dtype=np.float64) / float(l)
    return a / s


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -np.inf)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x - mx
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def soft_active_weights(conv, tau):
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64)
    if conv.size == 0:
        return conv
    if tau is None or tau <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    z = (conv - float(conv.max())) / tau
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weighted_corr(lam, f):
    """
    h[i] = sum_k lam[k] * f[k-i] over valid f indices.
    This is the derivative (up to factor 2) of lambda-weighted convolution.
    """
    L = len(lam)
    n = len(f)
    rev = lam[::-1]
    conv = lin_convolve(f, rev)
    # conv length is n + L - 1.  Needed indices are L-n, ..., L-1 reversed.
    return np.maximum(conv[L - n:L][::-1], 0.0)


def blend_line_search(f, g, curr_M, alphas=None):
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
    best_a = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_M = float(curr_M)
    best_conv = None
    for alpha in alphas:
        if alpha <= 0.0:
            continue
        a = (1.0 - alpha) * f + alpha * g
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a = a / s
        conv = fast_conv_same(a)
        M = float(conv.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_conv = conv
    if best_conv is None:
        best_conv = fast_conv_same(best_a)
        best_M = float(best_conv.max())
    return best_a, best_M, best_conv


def interpolate_sequence(parent, new_n):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if len(parent) == new_n:
        return parent.copy()
    if len(parent) == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(parent[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(parent))
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, parent)


def perturb_simplex(base, scale, seed):
    seed = int(seed) % 998244353
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    v = base + rng.standard_normal(len(base)) * scale
    return normalize_nonneg(v, 0.0)


def append_normalized(collection, v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        collection.append(v / s)
    return collection


def add_to_top(top, score, seq, max_keep=5):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    top.append((float(score), np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(top) > max_keep:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > top[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        top.pop(worst)


def try_candidate(state, a, label, force=False):
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    an = a / s
    conv = fast_conv_same(an)
    M = float(conv.max())
    direct = float(2.0 * an.size * M)
    if not np.isfinite(direct):
        return direct

    old_direct = state[3]
    if direct < state[3]:
        state[3] = direct

    do_eval = force or (state[0] is None) or (direct < old_direct - 1e-12)
    if do_eval:
        try:
            actual = float(evaluate_sequence(an.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            actual = direct
        if np.isfinite(actual) and (state[0] is None or actual < state[1]):
            state[0] = an.copy()
            state[1] = float(actual)
            state[2] = int(an.size)
            state[3] = direct
            print(f"NEW BEST [{label}] actual={state[1]:.8f} direct={direct:.8f} n={state[2]}")
        elif state[0] is None and np.isfinite(direct):
            state[0] = an.copy()
            state[1] = float(direct)
            state[2] = int(an.size)
            state[3] = direct
    return direct


def deterministic_candidates(n, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % 998244353)
    c = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    append_normalized(c, np.ones(n))

    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        append_normalized(c, (idx + 1.0) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (n - idx) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (idx + 1.0) ** p + 0.5 * (n - idx) ** p)

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        append_normalized(c, x ** p)
        append_normalized(c, (1.0 - x) ** p)
        append_normalized(c, x ** p + 0.5 * (1.0 - x) ** p)

    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7):
        for amp in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 0.99):
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x))
            append_normalized(c, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + 0.5))

    for alpha in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
        append_normalized(c, rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)))

    for _ in range(6):
        k = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
        amp = 0.3 + 0.5 * float(rng.random())
        phase = float(rng.random() * 6.28318)
        v = (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + phase)
             + 0.3 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (k + 1) * x + phase))
        append_normalized(c, v)

    d = np.minimum(x, 1.0 - x) + 1e-8
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 / (d ** p))

    append_normalized(c, np.exp(-16.0 * (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5)))

    for period in (2, 3, 5, 8):
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * idx / period))
        append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * idx / period))

    append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 - 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 + 0.7 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    append_normalized(c, 1.0 - 0.3 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    return c


def lse_mirror_optimize(a_init, time_limit, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.3,
                        seed=0, verbose=False):
    """Fast mirror descent with softmax/active-peak smoothing."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    tau = max(1e-12, tau_frac * M)
    ms = float(move_scale)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        it += 1
        p = soft_active_weights(conv, tau)
        h = weighted_corr(p, a)
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if hmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        eta = ms / hmax
        a_raw = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a_raw + 1e-12, 0.0)

        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(
            a, a_new, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
        )

        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                tau = max(1e-12, tau * 0.95)
                ms = min(1.0, ms * 1.02)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                tau = min(2.0 * M + 1e-12, tau * 1.05)
                ms = max(0.05, ms * 0.98)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            tau = min(2.0 * M + 1e-12, tau * 1.15)
            ms = max(0.05, ms * 0.90)
            if no_improve > 40:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 40) % 5)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                tau = max(1e-12, 0.1 * M)
                ms = 0.3
                no_improve = 0

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    LSE it={it} M={best_M:.10f}")

    return best, best_M


def game_sca_optimize(a_init, time_limit, temp_frac=0.2, inner_block=20,
                      beta=0.25, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    SCA outer iteration.  Inner matrix game approximates:
       min_{g in simplex} max_k (a*g)_k
    via multiplicative weights on payoff matrix F_{k,i}=a_{k-i}.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    size_g = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    size_l = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
    Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)

    g = a.copy()
    g_avg = a.copy()
    T = max(1e-12, float(temp_frac) * M)
    lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
    lam_avg = lam.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    outer = 0
    no_improve = 0
    alphas = (0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        for _ in range(max(1, int(inner_block))):
            Gg = np.fft.rfft(g, size_g)
            fg = np.fft.irfft(Af_g * Gg, size_g)[:L]
            row = softmax(fg / T)

            lamr = lam[::-1]
            Ll = np.fft.rfft(lamr, size_l)
            Rt = np.fft.irfft(Af_l * Ll, size_l)
            h = np.maximum(Rt[L - n:L][::-1], 0.0)

            col = softmax(-h / T)

            g = col
            lam = row
            lam_avg = (1.0 - beta) * lam_avg + beta * row
            g_avg = (1.0 - beta) * g_avg + beta * col

        g_use = normalize_nonneg(g_avg, 0.0)
        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, g_use, M, alphas=alphas)
        improved = False
        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            improved = True
        else:
            hmax = float(h.max())
            if hmax > 1e-300:
                eta = 0.2 / hmax
                a_mw = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(-eta * h) + 1e-12, 0.0)
                newa2, newM2, newconv2 = blend_line_search(
                    a, a_mw, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
                )
                if newM2 < M - 1e-15:
                    a, M, conv = newa2, newM2, newconv2
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and outer % 25 == 0:
                    print(f"    GAME outer={outer} M={best_M:.10f}")

            Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
            Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)
            temp_frac = max(0.05, float(temp_frac) * 0.95)
            T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)

            g = g_use.copy()
            g_avg = g_use.copy()
            lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
            lam_avg = lam.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            temp_frac = min(2.0, float(temp_frac) * 1.1)
            T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)
            if no_improve > 30:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.004 * (1 + (no_improve // 30) % 5)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + outer)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best = a.copy()
                Af_g = np.fft.rfft(a, size_g)
                Af_l = np.fft.rfft(a, size_l)
                temp_frac = 0.2
                T = max(1e-12, temp_frac * M)
                g = a.copy()
                g_avg = a.copy()
                lam = soft_active_weights(conv, T)
                lam_avg = lam.copy()
                no_improve = 0

        outer += 1

    return best, best_M


def build_conv_structure(n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = n * n
    rows = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    p = 0
    for k in range(L):
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(n - 1, k)
        c = end - start + 1
        if c > 0:
            rows[p:p + c] = k
            cols[p:p + c] = np.arange(start, end + 1, dtype=np.int32)
            p += c
    return rows, cols


def sca_lp_minmax_step(f, row_idx, col_idx, t_rows, t_cols):
    n = int(len(f))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    vals = f[row_idx - col_idx]
    data = np.concatenate((vals, -np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)))
    rr = np.concatenate((row_idx, t_rows))
    cc = np.concatenate((col_idx, t_cols))
    A_ub = csr_matrix((data, (rr, cc)), shape=(L, n + 1))
    b_ub = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    cobj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cobj[-1] = 1.0

    A_eq = csr_matrix((np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), (np.arange(n), np.arange(n))),
                      shape=(1, n + 1))
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)

    res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                  bounds=bounds, method="highs", options={"presolve": True})
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
        g = np.maximum(res.x[:n], 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            return g / s
    return None


def sca_lp_optimize(a_init, time_limit, seed=0, max_elems=1800000, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n * n > max_elems:
        return a, score_direct(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    row_idx, col_idx = build_conv_structure(n)
    t_rows = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)
    t_cols = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)

    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        g = None
        try:
            g = sca_lp_minmax_step(a, row_idx, col_idx, t_rows, t_cols)
        except Exception:
            g = None

        improved = False
        if g is not None:
            g = normalize_nonneg(g, 0.0)
            newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, g, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0))
            if newM < M - 1e-15:
                a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            p = soft_active_weights(conv, max(1e-12, 0.05 * M))
            h = weighted_corr(p, a)
            hmax = float(h.max())
            if hmax > 1e-300:
                eta = 0.2 / hmax
                a_mw = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(-eta * h) + 1e-12, 0.0)
                newa2, newM2, newconv2 = blend_line_search(
                    a, a_mw, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
                )
                if newM2 < M - 1e-15:
                    a, M, conv = newa2, newM2, newconv2
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"    LP it={it} M={best_M:.10f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 8) % 4)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    return best, best_M


def peak_shave_optimize(a_init, time_limit, seed=0, frac=0.05, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a_init, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        conv = fast_conv_same(a)
        M = float(conv.max())
        if M < best_M:
            best_M = M
            best = a.copy()

        k = int(np.argmax(conv))
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k)
        idx = np.arange(js, je + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if idx.size > 0:
            partner = k - idx
            contrib[idx] = 2.0 * a[idx] * a[partner]
        total = float(contrib.sum())
        if total <= 1e-300:
            break

        move = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        frac_cur = float(frac) * (1.0 + 0.05 * (it % 5))
        if idx.size > 0:
            move[idx] = -0.5 * frac_cur * a[idx] * (contrib[idx] / total)

        outside = np.concatenate((np.arange(0, js, dtype=np.int64),
                                  np.arange(je + 1, n, dtype=np.int64)))
        if outside.size == 0:
            avg = total / float(idx.size) if idx.size > 0 else 0.0
            low = idx[contrib[idx] < avg]
            if low.size == 0:
                no_improve += 1
                it += 1
                if no_improve > 20:
                    a = perturb_simplex(best, 0.003, seed + it)
                    conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                    M = float(conv.max())
                    no_improve = 0
                continue
            svals = float(a[low].sum())
            if svals > 1e-300:
                move[low] = -float(move.sum()) * a[low] / svals
            else:
                move[low] = -float(move.sum()) / float(low.size)
        else:
            total_move = -float(move.sum())
            if total_move > 0.0:
                vals = a[outside]
                svals = float(vals.sum())
                if svals > 1e-300:
                    move[outside] = total_move * vals / svals
                else:
                    move[outside] = total_move / float(outside.size)

        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a + move, 0.0)
        newa, newM, newconv = blend_line_search(a, a_new, M, alphas=(0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0))
        if newM < M - 1e-15:
            a, M, conv = newa, newM, newconv
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                scale = 0.002 + 0.003 * (1 + (no_improve // 25) % 4)
                a = perturb_simplex(best, scale, seed + it)
                conv = fast_conv_same(a)
                M = float(conv.max())
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    if M < best_M:
        best_M = M
        best = a.copy()
    return best, best_M


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 25.0
    seed = int(seed) % 998244353

    state = [None, float("inf"), 0, float("inf")]
    top = []

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
                parent = normalize_nonneg(p, 0.0)
                d = try_candidate(state, parent, "parent", force=True)
                add_to_top(top, d, parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n_list = []
    if parent is not None:
        n_list.append(int(parent.size))
    for n in (256, 384, 512, 640, 768, 896, 1024, 1280, 1536, 2048):
        n_list.append(n)

    unique_n = []
    for n in n_list:
        if 64 <= n <= 3072 and n not in unique_n:
            unique_n.append(int(n))
    unique_n.sort()

    for n in unique_n:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
            break
        if parent is not None:
            an = interpolate_sequence(parent, n)
        else:
            an = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        an = normalize_nonneg(an, 0.0)
        d = try_candidate(state, an, f"parent_n{n}")
        add_to_top(top, d, an)

        if n in (512, 640, 768, 896, 1024):
            cands = deterministic_candidates(n, seed + n)
            if len(cands) > 80:
                cands = cands[:80]
            for ci, cand in enumerate(cands):
                d = try_candidate(state, cand, f"cand_n{n}_{ci}")
                add_to_top(top, d, cand)
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
                    break
        else:
            for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 5.0):
                rng2 = np.random.default_rng((seed + 1000 * n + int(alpha * 100)) % 998244353)
                a = rng2.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha))
                d = try_candidate(state, a, "dir")
                add_to_top(top, d, a)

    if state[0] is None:
        a = np.ones(768, dtype=np.float64) / 768.0
        d = try_candidate(state, a, "uniform_fallback", force=True)
        add_to_top(top, d, a)

    # Symmetrize current top sequences.
    for _, a in list(top):
        s = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        s = normalize_nonneg(s, 0.0)
        d = try_candidate(state, s, "sym")
        add_to_top(top, d, s)

    # Pairwise blends among diverse top candidates.
    top_snapshot = list(top)
    for i in range(len(top_snapshot)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(top_snapshot)):
            a1 = top_snapshot[i][1]
            a2 = top_snapshot[j][1]
            if a1.shape != a2.shape:
                continue
            for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                b = alpha * a1 + (1.0 - alpha) * a2
                b = normalize_nonneg(b, 0.0)
                d = try_candidate(state, b, "blend")
                add_to_top(top, d, b)

    best_a = state[0].copy() if state[0] is not None else np.ones(768) / 768.0

    # Quick smooth mirror descent.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        t = min(80.0, rem - 250.0)
        if t > 10.0:
            res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(best_a, t, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.3,
                                           seed=seed, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lse1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Main SCA game optimization.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500.0:
        t = min(800.0, rem - 300.0)
        if t > 30.0:
            res_a, _ = game_sca_optimize(best_a, t, temp_frac=0.20,
                                         inner_block=20, beta=0.25,
                                         seed=seed + 10, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "game1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Second diverse basin, if available.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 250.0 and len(top) > 1:
        sec_a = top[1][1]
        t = min(150.0, rem - 150.0)
        if t > 20.0:
            res_a, _ = game_sca_optimize(sec_a, t, temp_frac=0.25,
                                         inner_block=20, beta=0.25,
                                         seed=seed + 20, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "game2", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Exact linearized LP polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        t = min(150.0, rem - 80.0)
        if t > 20.0:
            res_a, _ = sca_lp_optimize(best_a, t, seed=seed + 30, verbose=True)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lp1", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Final smooth polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        t = min(60.0, rem - 30.0)
        if t > 10.0:
            res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(best_a, t, tau_frac=0.02, move_scale=0.15,
                                           seed=seed + 40, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "lse2", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Peak shaving.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0:
        t = min(30.0, rem - 20.0)
        if t > 5.0:
            res_a, _ = peak_shave_optimize(best_a, t, seed=seed + 50, verbose=False)
            d = try_candidate(state, res_a, "peak", force=True)
            add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
            if state[0] is not None:
                best_a = state[0].copy()

    # Random basin hopping.
    restart = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        rng_r = np.random.default_rng((seed + 5000 + restart) % 998244353)
        if restart % 4 == 0 and len(top) > 0:
            idxb = int(rng_r.integers(0, len(top)))
            base = top[idxb][1]
        else:
            base = best_a
        scale = 0.008 + 0.02 * float(int(rng_r.integers(0, 6)))
        a = perturb_simplex(base, scale, seed + 10000 + restart)
        t = min(10.0, rem - 5.0)
        if t < 5.0:
            break
        res_a, _ = lse_mirror_optimize(a, t, tau_frac=0.05, move_scale=0.25,
                                       seed=seed + 20000 + restart, verbose=False)
        d = try_candidate(state, res_a, f"restart{restart}", force=False)
        add_to_top(top, d, res_a)
        if state[0] is not None:
            best_a = state[0].copy()
        restart += 1
        if restart >= 40:
            break

    # Final blends after all optimizers.
    top_snapshot = list(top)
    for i in range(len(top_snapshot)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(top_snapshot)):
            a1 = top_snapshot[i][1]
            a2 = top_snapshot[j][1]
            if a1.shape != a2.shape:
                continue
            b = 0.5 * a1 + 0.5 * a2
            b = normalize_nonneg(b, 0.0)
            d = try_candidate(state, b, "final_blend", force=False)
            add_to_top(top, d, b)

    if state[0] is not None:
        try:
            final_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(state[0].tolist()))
        except Exception:
            final_actual = state[1]
        if np.isfinite(final_actual) and final_actual < state[1]:
            state[1] = float(final_actual)
        print(f"FINAL score={state[1]:.8f} n={state[2]}")
        return [float(x) for x in state[0]]

    return [1.0 / 768.0] * 768
```
